def analyze_system [] {<\n>    # Get system processes with memory usage<\n>    let processes = (ps | sort-by cpu | last 10)<\n>    <\n>    # Get disk usage<\n>    let disk = (ls / | where type == "dir" | reduce -f {} {|it, acc|<\n>        let size = (du -d 0 ($it.name) | get apparent)<\n>        $acc | merge {($it.name): $size}<\n>    })<\n>    <\n>    # Get system stats<\n>    let sys = (sys | get host)<\n>    <\n>    # Get memory stats with conversion to GB<\n>    let mem = (sys | get memory | update total {|it| <\n>        $it.total / 1024 / 1024 / 1024 | math round -p 2<\n>    } | update used {|it| <\n>        $it.used / 1024 / 1024 / 1024 | math round -p 2<\n>    })<\n>    <\n>    # Combine all data into a structured report<\n>    {<\n>        timestamp: (date now | date format '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'),<\n>        system: $sys,<\n>        memory_gb: $mem,<\n>        top_processes: $processes,<\n>        directory_sizes: $disk<\n>    } | to json -i 2 | save system_report.json<\n>    <\n>    # Generate a summary view<\n>    echo $"(ansi green)System Report Generated!(ansi reset)"<\n>    echo $"Total Memory: ($mem.total) GB"<\n>    echo $"Used Memory: ($mem.used) GB"<\n>    echo "Top CPU Processes:"<\n>    $processes | select name cpu mem | first 5<\n>}

def --env get_system_stats [] {<\n>    {<\n>        top_cpu: (ps | sort-by cpu -r | first 5 | select pid name cpu mem | update mem {|it| $it.mem | into filesize}),<\n>        sys_load: (sys | get host.load_average),<\n>        total_mem: ((sys | get mem.total | into filesize) + " / " + (sys | get mem.free | into filesize) + " free"),<\n>        timestamp: (date now | date format '%H:%M:%S')<\n>    }<\n>}<\n><\n>def format_header [] { <\n>    $"(ansi rb)╭──── System Monitor ────────────────────────────╮(ansi reset)" <\n>}<\n><\n>def format_footer [ts: string] { <\n>    $"(ansi rb)╰──── Updated: ($ts) ────────────────────╯(ansi reset)" <\n>}<\n><\n>while true { <\n>    clear<\n>    let stats = (get_system_stats)<\n>    print (format_header)<\n>    print $"\n(ansi green)CPU Hungry Processes:(ansi reset)"<\n>    $stats.top_cpu | table<\n>    print $"\n(ansi yellow)System Load: ($stats.sys_load)(ansi reset)"<\n>    print $"(ansi cyan)Memory: ($stats.total_mem)(ansi reset)"<\n>    print (format_footer $stats.timestamp)<\n>    sleep 2sec <\n>}

#!/usr/bin/env rdmd<\n>module conv;<\n><\n>import std.stdio;<\n>import std.file;<\n>import std.string;<\n>import std.getopt;<\n>import std.conv;<\n>import std.regex;<\n>import std.algorithm;<\n><\n>// Simple PDF generator<\n>class PDFGenerator {<\n>private:<\n>    string content;<\n>    string title;<\n>    string author;<\n>    int fontSize = 12;<\n>    <\n>public:<\n>    this(string title = "", string author = "") {<\n>        this.title = title;<\n>        this.author = author;<\n>    }<\n>    <\n>    void addText(string text) {<\n>        content ~= "BT\n/F1 " ~ fontSize.to!string ~ " Tf\n";<\n>        foreach (line; text.splitLines()) {<\n>            content ~= "(" ~ escapeText(line) ~ ") Tj T*\n";<\n>        }<\n>        content ~= "ET\n";<\n>    }<\n>    <\n>    void setFontSize(int size) {<\n>        fontSize = size;<\n>    }<\n>    <\n>    ubyte[] generate() {<\n>        string pdf = "%PDF-1.4\n";<\n>        <\n>        // Add catalog<\n>        pdf ~= "1 0 obj\n<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>\nendobj\n";<\n>        <\n>        // Add pages<\n>        pdf ~= "2 0 obj\n<< /Type /Pages /Kids [3 0 R] /Count 1 >>\nendobj\n";<\n>        <\n>        // Add page content<\n>        pdf ~= "3 0 obj\n<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /Contents 4 0 R >>\nendobj\n";<\n>        <\n>        // Add stream<\n>        string stream = "<< /Length " ~ content.length.to!string ~ " >>\n";<\n>        stream ~= "stream\n" ~ content ~ "endstream\n";<\n>        pdf ~= "4 0 obj\n" ~ stream ~ "endobj\n";<\n>        <\n>        // Add font<\n>        pdf ~= "5 0 obj\n<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica >>\nendobj\n";<\n>        <\n>        // Add info<\n>        pdf ~= "6 0 obj\n<<";<\n>        if (!title.empty) pdf ~= " /Title (" ~ escapeText(title) ~ ")";<\n>        if (!author.empty) pdf ~= " /Author (" ~ escapeText(author) ~ ")";<\n>        pdf ~= " >>\nendobj\n";<\n>        <\n>        // Add xref<\n>        size_t xrefPos = pdf.length;<\n>        pdf ~= "xref\n0 7\n";<\n>        pdf ~= "0000000000 65535 f \n";<\n>        pdf ~= "0000000010 00000 n \n";<\n>        pdf ~= "0000000069 00000 n \n";<\n>        pdf ~= "0000000128 00000 n \n";<\n>        pdf ~= "0000000209 00000 n \n";<\n>        pdf ~= "0000000392 00000 n \n";<\n>        pdf ~= "0000000483 00000 n \n";<\n>        <\n>        // Add trailer<\n>        pdf ~= "trailer\n<< /Size 7 /Root 1 0 R /Info 6 0 R >>\n";<\n>        pdf ~= "startxref\n" ~ xrefPos.to!string ~ "\n%%EOF\n";<\n>        <\n>        return cast(ubyte[])pdf;<\n>    }<\n>    <\n>private:<\n>    string escapeText(string text) {<\n>        return text<\n>            .replace("\\", "\\\\")<\n>            .replace("(", "\\(")<\n>            .replace(")", "\\)");<\n>    }<\n>}<\n><\n>// Basic markdown to plain text conversion<\n>string mdToText(string md) {<\n>    string result = md;<\n>    <\n>    // Convert headers<\n>    result = result.replaceAll(regex(`^#+\s+(.*)$`, "gm"), "$1\n");<\n>    <\n>    // Convert bold/italic<\n>    result = result.replaceAll(regex(`\*\*(.*?)\*\*`, "g"), "$1");<\n>    result = result.replaceAll(regex(`\*(.*?)\*`, "g"), "$1");<\n>    result = result.replaceAll(regex(`_(.*?)_`, "g"), "$1");<\n>    <\n>    // Convert links<\n>    result = result.replaceAll(regex(`\[(.*?)\]\(.*?\)`, "g"), "$1");<\n>    <\n>    // Convert lists<\n>    result = result.replaceAll(regex(`^\s*[\*\+-]\s+(.*)$`, "gm"), "• $1\n");<\n>    result = result.replaceAll(regex(`^\s*\d+\.\s+(.*)$`, "gm"), "$1\n");<\n>    <\n>    return result;<\n>}<\n><\n>void main(string[] args) {<\n>    string inputFile;<\n>    string outputFile;<\n>    string title = "";<\n>    string author = "";<\n>    int fontSize = 12;<\n>    <\n>    getopt(args,<\n>        "input|i", "Input Markdown file", &inputFile,<\n>        "output|o", "Output PDF file", &outputFile,<\n>        "title|t", "Document title", &title,<\n>        "author|a", "Document author", &author,<\n>        "font-size|f", "Base font size", &fontSize<\n>    );<\n>    <\n>    if (inputFile.empty || outputFile.empty) {<\n>        stderr.writeln("Error: Both input and output files must be specified");<\n>        stderr.writeln("Usage: conv -i input.md -o output.pdf [options]");<\n>        stderr.writeln("Options:");<\n>        stderr.writeln("  -t, --title     Document title");<\n>        stderr.writeln("  -a, --author    Document author");<\n>        stderr.writeln("  -f, --font-size Base font size (default: 12)");<\n>        return;<\n>    }<\n>    <\n>    try {<\n>        // Read markdown file<\n>        string mdContent = readText(inputFile);<\n>        <\n>        // Convert markdown to plain text<\n>        string textContent = mdToText(mdContent);<\n>        <\n>        // Create PDF document<\n>        auto pdf = new PDFGenerator(title, author);<\n>        pdf.setFontSize(fontSize);<\n>        pdf.addText(textContent);<\n>        <\n>        // Save PDF<\n>        write(outputFile, pdf.generate());<\n>        <\n>        writeln("Successfully converted ", inputFile, " to ", outputFile);<\n>    } catch (Exception e) {<\n>        stderr.writeln("Error: ", e.msg);<\n>    }<\n>}
